Prior: v1.121.0 (2026-11-25, T-20261125-003): tombstone flags are previewable under --dry-run — the hard "apply-mode only" refusal is replaced by a read-only preview (tombstoneSkip reports the would-be TOMBSTONED_SKIP naming the matching grant; nothing is persisted, the manifest write stays apply-only), and a new grant-coverage WARN names a --tombstone glob that matched no candidate plus a --clear-tombstone glob matching no recorded tombstone/baseline. The dry-run TOMBSTONED row itself was verified already emitted at HEAD (the ticket's fidelity half did not reproduce); both behaviours are now pinned by tests/unit/upgrade-dry-run-tombstone.test.ts and the flipped expectation in tests/unit/upgrade-decision-log.test.ts. v1.120.0 (2026-11-25, PR #2177 Windows CI): the §4.6 a script-lane coverage sweep normalises its Bun.Glob entries with `toPosixPath()` — on Windows every delivered script read as unplaced (spurious UNCLASSIFIED WARN rows with `\` paths) and nested `SCRIPTS.md` files were named as unclassified scripts. v1.119.0 (2026-11-25, T-20261124-007 — design docs/designs/2026-11-25-upgrade-runner-machinery-batch-design.md §4.7): `.github/gitleaks-full.toml` joins the LOCKED always-take-template list — the full-scan secrets config reaches every fleet repository as a byte copy of the template file. v1.118.0 (2026-11-25, T-20261124-013 / U-20261123-003 — design docs/designs/2026-11-25-upgrade-runner-machinery-batch-design.md §4.6): four script-lane gaps — (a) the common script lane also walks `.py`, and every file under the two script source roots that no lane places gets an UNCLASSIFIED (WARN) row; (b) after the last script pass a lockstep loop compares each template-equal delivered script's @version with its scripts/SCRIPTS.md row, reconciles once, and records REGISTRY_DRIFT (ERROR) when the row still disagrees (a registry table with no data row yet can receive its first appended row); (c) scriptRowGate skips an L0-only-row script with L0_ONLY_SKIP (INFO) and the L0-to-L3 layer rewrite on append is removed; (d) an appended registry row escapes pipes inside backtick code spans. v1.117.0 (2026-11-25, design docs/designs/2026-11-25-upgrade-runner-machinery-batch-design.md §4.8): `evolvedDeliveryGuard` delivers a CONTENT_EQUIVALENT candidate (decided in upgrade-policy.ts v1.50.0) with verdict CONTENT_EQUIVALENT at INFO — no adopt grant; `markerLossBlocks` runs first, so a project marker the template lacks still blocks. v1.116.0 (2026-11-23, U-20261122-011 — spec docs/designs/2026-11-23-variant-core-script-loss-detection-design.md §5-§6): loss attribution + eviction transparency + legacy core-delta scan — (a) every MARKER_LOSS row detail and the adopt-grant override ADOPT row name the lost LOCAL-PATCH ticket ids (lostLocalPatchIds; "2 → 0" becomes attributable to filed upstream tickets); (b) every deliver path over differing project content (UNBASELINED_OVERWRITE, adopt-override of MARKER_LOSS, ADOPT over PROJECT_EVOLVED, equal-version unproven-fork ADOPT, both core-locked canonical restores) carries a capped evictedDelta listing vs the recorded delivery baseline (baseline text recovered from the manifest blob id, else hash-matched against template git history; unrecoverable = no listing — no false positives by construction; vintage-proven copies never list: the UNTOUCHED and vintage short-circuits are preserved) and the exit summary gains an EVICTED LINES block grouped by path; verdict/severity matrix byte-identical; (c) new read-only, report-only --audit-core-deltas [--project <path>]... — diffs CORE_LOCKED_SCRIPTS copies (const hoisted to the CLI front, shared with the SYNC_IF_NEWER pass) against their recorded delivery baselines and reports the PROJECT-AUTHORED DELTA with marker ids, exiting 0 before any gate/stash/pass runs (zero false positives by construction: content equal to the last delivered baseline cannot be variant-authored). Unit-pinned by tests/unit/upgrade-policy-eviction.test.ts. Prior: v1.115.0 (2026-11-21, spec docs/designs/2026-11-21-co-utility-dedup-and-adoption-role-design.md §5.3-§5.5): adoption-only engine gate — fail-closed refusal for targets whose variant.json declares consumption:"adoption-only" (lazy flag-present-only read, tenant-guard §3.4 pattern; refusal-grade, not --yes-bypassable); per-invocation --confirm-curated unlock with dry-run default until --confirm (two explicit tokens) + --surfaces claim-class selection validated against the run's upgrade-policy claims, narrowing the claim-classed delivery passes. Prior: v1.114.0 (2026-11-20, T-20261120-003 — spec docs/designs/2026-11-20-upgrade-adopt-row-truthfulness-design.md): VARIANT-SCRIPTS WALK-ABORT FIX — DECISION ROWS NOW COVER EVERY OWNED PATH. The nested `syncVariantScripts` walk aborted on the FIRST evolvedDeliveryGuard conflict-skip (`if (!proceeded) return;` in both the versioned and the unversioned arm returned out of the recursive walk function), so every scripts/<variant>/ file visited after the first skip got NO decision row, NO CONFLICT/SKIP line and NO copy from the variant channel (the 2026-11-19 co-deck 0.30.0 wave: only auto-calibrate.ts carried a VARIANT SCRIPTS row, 20 script-family paths swallowed, .mjs/.json surfaces lost both channels); an adopt-template grant was honored right up to the abort — accepted, then dropped. Both arms now use the overlay arm's `if (proceeded)` shape: a guard-skip skips ONE file, the walk continues, every owned path gets exactly one truthful row (sole-increment contract kept). Also truthful: the VARIANT OVERLAY arm syncChanged++ no longer counts conflict-skipped files (moved inside if(proceeded)); the common scripts pass equal-version and CORE-LOCKED restore arms print the delivered project path (scripts/...) instead of the bare rel key in DRIFT/FORK/COPIED lines and pass relPath to reconcileScriptRegistry (same registry resolution — report-only). Verified-not-a-bug (row-vs-copy audit, no change): PROJECT_EVOLVED and equalVersionRestore arms run markerLossBlocks BEFORE recordDecision; every NO_BASELINE deliver=true outcome reaches its copy; PROJECT_AHEAD under a grant stays PROJECT_AHEAD. Unit-pinned by tests/unit/upgrade-variant-scripts-walk.test.ts (negative control: 3 of 5 assertions fail pre-fix). Prior: v1.113.0 (2026-11-20, T-20261120-001 — design docs/designs/2026-11-20-adoption-gaps-batch-design.md, U-20261119-002 gap 3): VARIANT-SCOPE SKILL PRUNE gains a leading source-variant adoption-residue arm — reads the adoption provenance (.claude/adopt-project-decisions.json) and, when the project variant.json identity has been flipped away from the adoption variant, prunes proven source-variant-seeded skills (six-way conjunction: source-template-declared AND present AND not in the current project manifest AND not a recorded foreign skill AND not target-declared AND not common-owned) from skills/ + PLATFORM_SKILL_BASES mirrors, deletionMarkerLossBlocks-guarded, decision-logged DELETE rows; identity not flipped → loud INFO only; pure selection in helpers/adoption-provenance.ts (unit-tested), fail-safe on any missing/malformed input. Prior: v1.112.0 (2026-11-19, T-20261119-005 residual + T-20261119-009 — spec docs/designs/2026-11-19-delivery-riding-records-design.md): DELIVERY-RIDING RECORDS AND REGISTRY ROWS. R1: a delivered skills/<name>/SKILL.md (NEW/UPDATE arm, version-gated or hash-based, common + variant passes) reconciles the project's docs/lifecycle/skills/<name>.md in the same pass via reconcileSkillLifecycleRecord — generated-marker records refresh to the template projection (the marker's own contract), hand-authored records get surgical Version/Owner line alignment only (the alignSkillRegistryRowsWithFrontmatter precedent; the record is never rewritten wholesale), an absent record stays with the WORKSPACE seeding channel; decisions land as RECORD_BUMP (INFO), derivation failure (unresolvable owner) is WARN with remediation and no write. R2: reconcileScriptRegistry consults the variant sub-registry (templates/<variant>/scripts/<variant>/SCRIPTS.md, unique bare-basename match — the deck/ppt-master-adapter precedent row) after L0 and L1 common and synthesizes the 8-column main-registry row in-pass (SCRIPT_ROW_SYNTHESIZED, INFO); a MAIN-registry-governed delivered script with no upstream row anywhere FAILS LOUD via the pre-copy scriptRowGate — the copy is skipped fail-closed with an UNREGISTERED_SCRIPT (ERROR) row + remediation, so an upgrade can no longer ship the "Unregistered script" audit failure; paths under scripts/<variant>/ stay governed by the sub-registry that ships alongside them (verify-scripts walkScripts rule). Fleet survey (origin/main @ e6dd04ab1): zero currently-delivered unregistered main-governed scripts; co-deck 21 / co-consult 7 / co-work 2 sub-registry-only rows all resolve through the fallback. Spec-registry standing seeds evaluated and DROPPED: E5 (sync-only) is acceptable-by-design for pure sync deliveries — the relevance gate is recency-based (7d) so no static seed can satisfy it durably. Fixture-proven on a disposable /tmp co-work copy (committed surgery; pre-upgrade stash reverted the first uncommitted attempt): synth rows landed for deck/ppt-master-adapter + humanize/humanize-adapter, metadata-align inserted Version + aligned Owner in a hand-authored record, refresh-template overwrote a stale generated record. Tests: tests/unit/delivery-riding.test.ts (30). Prior: v1.111.0 (2026-11-19, T-20261118-005 — spec docs/designs/2026-11-18-pin-divergence-symmetry-design.md §5-§6): EXTERNAL DEPS pass (D3) pin-divergence symmetry — Arm 2 seed-target adoption (resolveSeedTarget max-rule; compatibility_test from the PROJECT root at the seed ref; pass moves pin+commit ADOPT, fail holds + WARN + failures++; dry-run emits an UPDATE would-run row) and Arm 1 SEED_LAG report rows on the dep's FINAL pin state (project-ahead / non-semver divergence → SEED_LAG WARN; same-pin commit divergence / repo mismatch → SKIP WARN; in-sync/project-behind → no row); rows never write, never move a seed, never increment changed/failures; the seed is parsed once next to the channel parses (invalid seed → absent, 2c precedent). Prior: v1.110.0 (2026-11-17, T-20261117-001, spec docs/designs/2026-11-17-root-machinery-batch-design.md §3): EXTERNAL DEPS pass channel 2c — SEED-ID ADOPTION. The pass had three write channels (absent-file seed copy, parse-failure reconcile, v1→v2 migration) and none fired for a VALID schema-v2 manifest missing template-seed ids (the co-deck/quickdl drift class, fixed one-off by co-deck PR #248). Channel 2c fires after the v1→v2 migration when the manifest parsed successfully and a valid seed exists: the pure adoptSeedEntries core (helpers/external-deps-reconcile.ts v1.2.0) adopts missing seed ids add-if-missing, verbatim; existing project entries are never touched; zero adoptions → no row, no write (byte-identical pass for the fleet majority). One UPDATE decision row per adopted id ("seed-id adoption"); --dry-run prints rows and writes nothing. 2c runs BEFORE the D5 gitignore coverage so adopted install_dirs get `external/<dir>/` coverage on arrival. No CLI reconcile subcommand (owner default O-5 — the upgrade pass is the only adoption vehicle). L0-only: no templates/common mirror. Tests: tests/unit/external-deps-reconcile-adoption.test.ts. Registry path seam CO_WORKSPACE_TENANT_LOOP_REGISTRY_FILE (absolute registry FILE path) replaces the repo-root path at the single point lib/fleet.ts tenantLoopRegistryPath — tests keep fixture registries in mkdtemp dirs, never the real repo gitignored state (T-20261104-003 discipline). Prior: v1.97.0 (2026-11-08, spec docs/designs/2026-11-08-gw-tenant-loop-and-hardening-design.md §3.4, WP1): tenant upgrade guard — when the resolved target matches a REGISTERED tenant (lib/fleet.ts tenantForProjectPath, realpath prefix match on the gateway's .pipeline-state/tenant-loop-registry.json export) the guard fires BEFORE any write in every mode: registry status must be active, scopes.upgrade must be true, and isTenantWithinWindows() must hold at execution time (the §4.3 double-enforcement, the same fleet.ts helper the gateway checks server-side); every failed condition prints as a WARN run-log row with the tenant id + reason and the run refuses with the conditions named. Tenant mode defaults to --dry-run unless the NEW --confirm flag is passed (deliberately distinct from --yes, the outside-Projects prompt consent, untouched; --confirm alone executes, --confirm on a non-tenant target is a WARN'd no-op). A guard-passing tenant target skips the outside-Projects --yes prompt — the registry gates + --confirm are that target's consent (tenants live outside Projects/ by construction). Fail-closed registry handling: a tenant registry that exists but fails to load (malformed) refuses outside-Projects targets naming the registry; absent/stale/schema-major mean zero tenants and leave non-tenant behavior byte-identical; inside-Projects targets stay governed by the fleet membership guard only (namespace separation, design §2). The TEMPLATE TREE SYNC pass gains a dedicated branch (modeled on JSON_MERGE, via lib/upgrade-policy v1.32.0 mergeWorkspaceSchemaJson) replacing the whole-content SYNC copy + conflict-skip path for exactly this file: template-owned blocks (platform_surfaces, routeAudit — absent from the project) and the $schema/version/description metadata scalars land verbatim, project-owned blocks are preserved, shared blocks leaf-merge project-wins with arrays atomic (never unioned). The content-level evolvedDeliveryGuard CONFLICT (ERROR) is bypassed on this path by design — committed project evolution is exactly what the merge absorbs; the uncommitted-local-modifications CONFLICT (WARN) guard stays (pre-upgrade stash covers rollback). Every withheld template change (a shared leaf whose project value won) logs one TEMPLATE_DELTA decision row (WARN for scalar/array divergences, INFO otherwise) — the decision log owns visibility of what did not land; the upgrade stays green. `--disposition adopt-template=docs/workspace-schema.json` remains the wholesale-adopt escape hatch (ADOPT row; template bytes verbatim; the merge bypassed). Malformed JSON on either side hard-SKIPs with an explicit error (SKIP row — no silent fallback to the whole-content copy). Dry-run parity is inherent: both sides are read from disk, nothing is written, and the MERGE verdict lists landed/preserved/withheld (verified on both halted projects: co-price MERGE landed routeAudit+platform_surfaces with 3 withheld designLint deltas, 131-entry allowlist predicted to survive; co-architect MERGE landed routeAudit with 2 withheld deltas, 122-entry allowlist predicted to survive). tests/unit/workspace-schema-merge.test.ts (design §5's 8 fixtures). Prior: v1.84.0 (2026-10-29, spec docs/designs/2026-10-29-delivery-bookkeeping-engine-design.md, DEC-20261029-01 — PR1 of five): delivery bookkeeping engine, four groups in one engine unit. (A1) NESTED LIFECYCLE RE-STAMP: the post-delivery re-stamp applies BOTH change-date primitives to each delivered ∩ git-dirty file — the existing top-level bumpFrontmatterLastUpdated AND the new bumpNestedLifecycleLastUpdated (helpers/upgrade-versions.ts v1.2.0) — so a version-gated delivery no longer leaves `lifecycle.last_updated` claiming a pre-delivery date (the co-abap staleness class, ~29 audit warnings fleet-wide per wave); `last_reviewed` is NEVER engine-stamped (DEC-20261029-01 condition 1); the T-20261028-002 deliveredRels ∩ git-dirty restriction stays as the honesty guard. (A3) OPT-IN CHANGELOG SCAFFOLDING: new `--changelog-entry` flag (apply-mode only, default OFF — CHANGELOG.md is project-owned) inserts one editable chore(upgrade) bullet at the top of the project CHANGELOG's first [Unreleased] section (section created when absent) via scaffoldChangelogEntry; idempotent per template version; runs BEFORE the manifest write so the CHANGELOG change rides the recorded delivery (files[] attribution; PIPELINE_ARTIFACT_PATHS membership keeps dev-sync step 3.9 tolerant). (B1) TOMBSTONE_CLEARED (INFO, lib v1.31.0): an applied --clear-tombstone grant that deletes ≥ 1 manifest entry records an auditable decision row naming the glob and the dropped paths; the shipped baseline-drop semantics (v1.79.0, 94a390945) are unchanged and pinned by regression tests (clear → next run absent-copy delivers as NEW, glob-exact drop scope, tombstoneGuard no-fire). (B2) TOMBSTONE PRUNE SYMMETRY: the tombstones carry-forward prunes entries whose template counterpart left the template tree — the exact mirror of the baselines rule (v1.79.0); a template-departed tombstone is provably inert. (D1) STUB_VERSION_DIVERGENCE (INFO, lib v1.31.0): the agents/ STUB skip arm reports a stub whose existing `version` differs from its template counterpart's (backfill never overwrites a sanctioned pin); placed after the primary skip rows so a consumer reading a path's first decision row still sees the verdict. lib/upgrade-policy.ts v1.31.0; helpers/upgrade-versions.ts v1.2.0; tests/unit/upgrade-restamp.test.ts v1.1.0 + tests/unit/upgrade-decision-log.test.ts v1.4.0. Prior: v1.79.0 (2026-10-28, T-20261028-014 — U-20261028-001 D4 integration defect): BASELINES CARRY-FORWARD at manifest write — the writer rebuilt baselines from the delivered set alone (since T-20261027-004 D1), dropping provenance for every path not delivered this run (conflict-skipped / tombstoned / disposition-skipped); the next run classified them NO_BASELINE and the agents/ equal-version CONFLICT (ERROR) preservation degraded into the overwrite-policy §8 migration-window WARN-deliver (proven end-to-end by the 0.22.1 co-design canary: the conflict-skipped agents/i18n-specialist.md lost its baseline and the follow-up dry-run showed DRIFT-restore WARN — the next wave would clobber the project-canonical phases:[4] at WARN). Rule mirrors the D1 tombstones carry-forward exactly: baselines = (prior ∪ freshly delivered) − paths delivered this run; delivered paths already receive fresh baselines; prior entries whose path left the template tree are pruned (never a classification candidate again). schemaVersion stays 2; the reader side (priorBaselines, classifyProjectState) is unchanged. Prior: v1.78.0 (2026-10-28, U-20261028-001 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-28-delivery-tombstone-ownership-design.md; PR-1a + PR-1b as ONE engine change set, DEC-20261028-02): delivery tombstones + agents/ project-ownership escalation. (D1) TOMBSTONE MANIFEST KEY: the delivery manifest v2 gains the additive optional tombstones key (schemaVersion stays 2) — { [rel]: { hash, templateVersion, recordedAt, reason } } — with the carry-forward rule `tombstones = (prior ∪ newly recorded) − paths delivered this run` (delivering a path is the natural clear event; directory rels expand recursively so a whole-skill delivery clears its sub-file tombstones). (D2/D3) TOMBSTONE GUARD: tombstoneSkip() consults lib/upgrade-policy.ts tombstoneGuard in EVERY absent-copy delivery branch BEFORE the copy — TEMPLATE TREE SYNC SYNC-absent / WORKSPACE seed / ADD_IF_MISSING seed / JSON_MERGE seed, DOCS_OVERWRITE, COMMANDS_SYNC, agents/ NEW, skills/ whole-directory NEW (common + variant passes), VARIANT ASSET DIRS, ENV_SAMPLE. First observation (baseline without a tombstone) logs TOMBSTONED (ERROR) — delivery refused, run exits non-zero (design §4.4 acceptance shape); recorded tombstones log TOMBSTONED_SKIP (INFO). Resolution flags: --tombstone <glob> (repeatable, apply-mode only — records the deletion as intended with --tombstone-reason; the confirmed run honors it immediately) and --clear-tombstone <glob> (drops entries so the next run delivers the path as NEW). Per D3b --disposition adopt-template does NOT override a tombstone — the guard never reads the disposition flag. (D4) AGENTS OWNERSHIP: the agents/ version-gt delivery arm's EVOLVED (WARN) + deliver row is reclassified to CONFLICT (ERROR) regardless of templateChanged (--disposition adopt-template still grants the delivery as ADOPT per the §5 disposition table), and the NO_BASELINE equal-version restore's decision detail names the migration window ("baseline absent — verify this restore against project intent"). (D6) NO resolver/classifier change — resolveClaim, the baseline classifier shapes, and the severity contract's existing rows are untouched. Dry-run reports candidates and persists nothing (the manifest stays apply-mode-only); resync-audit.ts v1.6.0 consumes the census. Prior: v1.77.0 (2026-10-28, T-20261028-002 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-28-ticket-runner-mechanical-batch-design.md §3): POST-DELIVERY RE-STAMP scope — the last_updated re-stamp walks deliveredRels (expanded to files, same recursion as the manifest baseline expansion) intersected with git status instead of every dirty worktree file, so pre-existing user changes open during an upgrade keep their dates; directory-delivered skill roots keep full coverage via the expansion. Prior: v1.76.0 (2026-10-27, T-20261027-004 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-27-sync-overwrite-policy-design.md §4–§7): sync/overwrite policy. (D1) PROVENANCE BASELINES: the delivery manifest (schemaVersion 2, `files` array verbatim) gains per-file baselines {hash, templateVersion} keyed by baselineKeyFor — the next run classifies every version-gated whole-content candidate as UNTOUCHED / IN_SYNC / PROJECT_EVOLVED / NO_BASELINE instead of keying on git-dirty state, so committed project divergence (the U-20261026-005 genus) is mechanically detectable. (D2) DISPOSITION MATRIX: PROJECT_EVOLVED + template-changed under TEMPLATE TREE SYNC / DOCS_OVERWRITE (phase-definitions + security) / VARIANT ASSET DIRS / ENV_SAMPLE / scripts/ + variant-scripts + overlay version-gt / agents/ + skills/ version-gt escalates to CONFLICT severity ERROR — default SKIP (project copy preserved, delivery skipped, decision recorded, non-zero exit with an actionable summary naming the §6 adjudicated-port procedure); `--disposition adopt-template[=glob]` delivers + re-baselines; template-unchanged evolution delivers as EVOLVED (WARN); NO_BASELINE degrades to today's behavior (design §8 rollout — no fleet red). (D3) UNIFIED DECISION LOG: every pass records {path, policy, pass, verdict, severity, baseline?, detail} — SEED_SKIP / STUB_SKIP / SKELETON_COLLISION (D6b, parsed from the skeleton lib's guarded-skip lines) / CONTEXT_PRESERVE / DRIFT_PRESERVE / DRIFT_RESTORED / MERGE / SKIP join the delivery verdicts; manifest v2 persists decisions[] and the composed PR body gains a Decisions section (counts by severity, ERROR items verbatim). agents/ equal-version drift on a PROJECT_EVOLVED file reclassifies to CONFLICT (ERROR) per the design's dedicated row; skills/ DRIFT (preserved) logs DRIFT_PRESERVE (WARN); scripts/ equal-version + CORE-SCRIPT FORK keep the ADR-0085 D3 canonical restore, logged DRIFT_RESTORED (WARN). T-20261027-005 integration: TIER NORMALIZED / TIER DRIFT log ADOPT (INFO), the MERGE-pass TIER CONFLICT keep logs EVOLVED (WARN). Dry-run classifies + logs without writing and previews the non-zero exit. Prior: v1.75.0 (2026-10-27, T-20261027-005): ADR-0102 legacy agent tier normalization in two delivery passes — (a) the MERGE pass normalizes the project agents/pm.md legacy per-platform tier frontmatter block to the template's scalar tier after the managed-block merge (tier block ONLY; a project scalar differing from the template scalar is deliberate divergence → TIER CONFLICT line, untouched); (b) the agents/ pass processes a variant extends-stub's `extends:` target (the resolved template-side counterpart the scaffold wrote into the project) instead of dead-ending, so common-side agent updates reach stub-shielded agents (the 13 variant i18n-specialist.md files; MERGE_MANAGED files stay excluded — their body delivery is the MERGE pass's contract, the v1.49.0 clobber class), and the equal-version drift reconcile recognizes tier-block-only drift with a focused TIER DRIFT verdict before delivering the template copy. Normalizer: lib/upgrade-policy.ts normalizeAgentTierFrontmatter (v1.24.0, pure + idempotent). Prior: v1.74.0 (2026-10-27, U-20261026-009): POST-DELIVERY RE-STAMP — after every content pass and before the delivery manifest, changed files (git status = content actually changed; version-touch-only writes stay clean and are skipped) get their top-level frontmatter `last_updated` advanced to the delivery date via helpers/upgrade-versions.ts bumpFrontmatterLastUpdated — upgrade deliveries mechanically rewrote agent bodies without advancing the date, so agent-lifecycle-audit emitted stale-date warnings after every wave (8/9 agent files warned 2026-10-26). Non-fatal on failure. Prior: v1.69.0 (2026-10-10, eight-platform coverage): list-valued `platform=` parsed via parsePlatformList (MERGE_FILES = union; `both` = all). v1.68.0 (T-20261007-028 + T-20261007-018): --preflight (clean tree / open PRs / template-version.txt, exit 0/1, no mutation); the delivery manifest write composes .claude/last-upgrade-pr-body.md at delivery time (stale-carried-body class). v1.67.0 (T-20261006-009 + T-20261006-010): PRUNE REMOVED agents/ consults the root agents/ SSOT (no more wrong "project-owned" basis); the registry-curated deletion exemption documented at v1.64.0 and the pass ordering pinned by test. v1.66.0 (2026-10-07, spec docs/designs/2026-10-07-registry-provenance-normalization-design.md): new POST-UPGRADE PROVENANCE NORMALIZE pass — after the scripts-snapshot regeneration, normalize-registry-provenance.ts relabels fossil provenance stamps in the project scripts/SCRIPTS.md (variant-delivered rows to `L2 · L2-only`, project-local rows to `L3 · L3`). Prior: v1.98.1 (2026-11-09, rogue-commit incident): all git spawns go through one local spawnSync shim injecting gitCleanEnv(), so fixture seeding/baselines/snapshots can never be redirected into the invoking repository by an inherited GIT_DIR. v1.98.0 (2026-11-09, CI fix PR #1935): tenant-loop registry read is lazy and path-scoped — only a target whose real path contains a gw-<12hex> segment consults .pipeline-state/tenant-loop-registry.json; every other outside-Projects target performs ZERO registry IO, so a stale/malformed registry can no longer fail an ordinary upgrade (the PR #1935 CI incident: a sibling suite left a malformed repo-root registry and later upgrade-project spawns inherited the fail-closed refusal). Registry path seam CO_WORKSPACE_TENANT_LOOP_REGISTRY_FILE (absolute registry FILE path) replaces the repo-root path at the single point lib/fleet.ts tenantLoopRegistryPath — tests keep fixture registries in mkdtemp dirs, never the real repo gitignored state (T-20261104-003 discipline). Refusal messages on plausible-tenant paths byte-identical to v1.97.0. Prior: v1.97.0 (2026-11-08, spec docs/designs/2026-11-08-gw-tenant-loop-and-hardening-design.md §3.4, WP1): tenant upgrade guard — when the resolved target matches a REGISTERED tenant (lib/fleet.ts tenantForProjectPath, realpath prefix match on the gateway's .pipeline-state/tenant-loop-registry.json export) the guard fires BEFORE any write in every mode: registry status must be active, scopes.upgrade must be true, and isTenantWithinWindows() must hold at execution time (the §4.3 double-enforcement, the same fleet.ts helper the gateway checks server-side); every failed condition prints as a WARN run-log row with the tenant id + reason and the run refuses with the conditions named. Tenant mode defaults to --dry-run unless the NEW --confirm flag is passed (deliberately distinct from --yes, the outside-Projects prompt consent, untouched; --confirm alone executes, --confirm on a non-tenant target is a WARN'd no-op). A guard-passing tenant target skips the outside-Projects --yes prompt — the registry gates + --confirm are that target's consent (tenants live outside Projects/ by construction). Fail-closed registry handling: a tenant registry that exists but fails to load (malformed) refuses outside-Projects targets naming the registry; absent/stale/schema-major mean zero tenants and leave non-tenant behavior byte-identical; inside-Projects targets stay governed by the fleet membership guard only (namespace separation, design §2). Prior: v1.91.0 (2026-11-07, spec docs/designs/2026-11-07-external-deps-machinery-design.md — implements the upstream external-dependency machinery): new EXTERNAL DEPS pass AFTER the TEMPLATE TREE SYNC pass (D3) — per dependency: git ls-remote upstream tag check (semver-aware; a tag not newer than the pin short-circuits to "current" + pin-drift tripwire report) → compatibility_test from the project root on a newer tag → pin move (manifest pin+commit rewrite + install_dir re-fetch) on pass, hold + WARN on fail; a decision-log row per dependency. --dry-run compatible (reports what it WOULD do, touches nothing). NEW --external-deps-only flag runs JUST this pass (the D2 scaffold-retry path; exit 1 on failures). The pass also seeds external-deps.json from the template seed when absent and reconciles a legacy/interim manifest shape into the generic schema (D1/D8 — the co-work LOCAL-PATCH instance retires on the next real run), validates install_dir .gitignore coverage with auto-append (D5), and TEMPLATE TREE SYNC / VARIANT ASSET DIRS hard-skip any path under a manifest install_dir (D4). Core in lib/external-deps.ts v1.0.0. Prior: v1.90.0 (2026-11-03, spec docs/designs/2026-11-01-hub-consolidation-design.md §15.4, ticket T-20261103-003-2): hub-consumption preflight legs — after the templatesDir invariants and before the --preflight apply-blockers block, upgrade runs the SAME two legs new-project runs at create time (checkConsumerMinimumVersions over the template variant.json hub block + checkHubDoctrineDuplication over templatesDir) via the shared helpers/hub-topology.ts dynamic import (one derivation, no copied logic). Guarded by !commonOnlySync (common-only sync delivers no variant assets) and skipped when the workspace has no valid hub_topology section (fail-open on registry absence). Read-only over the template tree and registry — runs unconditionally in BOTH --dry-run and apply mode; a FAIL prints each violation and exits 1 before any delivery-plan computation. Verified: dry-run fails closed on co-abap (3 residual unregistered duplications, T-20261103-008), passes co-price, skips under common-only self-declared identity. Prior: v1.87.0 (2026-10-31, design docs/designs/2026-10-31-graft-trail-migration-design.md R2.4, U-20261031-009): the two trail-migration upgrade-pass tolerance rules — (a) JSON_MERGE hook-entry guard: the template side of the settings merge is pre-filtered via dropMissingPathHookEntries (lib/hook-dedupe.ts v1.1.0) so template hook entries whose command references a repo-relative path absent from the project are dropped (existsSync-style guard on the helper path; GUARD line + MERGE-row detail), (b) retired-seed guard: with `.trail/` present (the trail-init migration marker) the graft-era seeds .mcp.json / .claude/helpers/graft-{hooks,statusline}.cjs are withheld from absent-copy delivery and decision-logged SEED_SKIP (retired-surface) via isTrailRetiredSeed (lib/upgrade-policy.ts v1.33.0); marker-less projects keep byte-for-byte behavior. tests/unit/graft-trail-migration.test.ts + upgrade-tree-sync.test.ts v1.5.0 TRAIL MIGRATION TOLERANCE fixtures. Prior: v1.86.0 (2026-10-31, spec docs/designs/2026-10-31-template-security-hardening-design.md R2.6 arm 1, U-20261031-008): the JSON_MERGE pass collapses exact-duplicate (event, matcher, command) hook entries in .claude/settings.json via lib/hook-dedupe.ts (dedupeClaudeSettingsHooks) before the merged payload lands — the array union previously accumulated variant graft-install hook blocks (co-consult/co-deck 5-SessionStart drift); the normalization applies even when the merge itself is a no-op, so already-accumulated project copies heal on their next upgrade visit; first occurrence wins, distinct commands preserved, cross-event repeats untouched. Prior: v1.85.0 (2026-10-31, spec docs/designs/2026-10-31-workspace-schema-merge-delivery-design.md): SCHEMA_MERGE delivery for docs/workspace-schema.json — the co-price 0.22.1→0.25.0 / co-architect 0.23.0→0.25.0 upgrade halts. The TEMPLATE TREE SYNC pass gains a dedicated branch (modeled on JSON_MERGE, via lib/upgrade-policy v1.32.0 mergeWorkspaceSchemaJson) replacing the whole-content SYNC copy + conflict-skip path for exactly this file: template-owned blocks (platform_surfaces, routeAudit — absent from the project) and the $schema/version/description metadata scalars land verbatim, project-owned blocks are preserved, shared blocks leaf-merge project-wins with arrays atomic (never unioned). The content-level evolvedDeliveryGuard CONFLICT (ERROR) is bypassed on this path by design — committed project evolution is exactly what the merge absorbs; the uncommitted-local-modifications CONFLICT (WARN) guard stays (pre-upgrade stash covers rollback). Every withheld template change (a shared leaf whose project value won) logs one TEMPLATE_DELTA decision row (WARN for scalar/array divergences, INFO otherwise) — the decision log owns visibility of what did not land; the upgrade stays green. `--disposition adopt-template=docs/workspace-schema.json` remains the wholesale-adopt escape hatch (ADOPT row; template bytes verbatim; the merge bypassed). Malformed JSON on either side hard-SKIPs with an explicit error (SKIP row — no silent fallback to the whole-content copy). Dry-run parity is inherent: both sides are read from disk, nothing is written, and the MERGE verdict lists landed/preserved/withheld (verified on both halted projects: co-price MERGE landed routeAudit+platform_surfaces with 3 withheld designLint deltas, 131-entry allowlist predicted to survive; co-architect MERGE landed routeAudit with 2 withheld deltas, 122-entry allowlist predicted to survive). tests/unit/workspace-schema-merge.test.ts (design §5's 8 fixtures). Prior: v1.84.0 (2026-10-29, spec docs/designs/2026-10-29-delivery-bookkeeping-engine-design.md, DEC-20261029-01 — PR1 of five): delivery bookkeeping engine, four groups in one engine unit. (A1) NESTED LIFECYCLE RE-STAMP: the post-delivery re-stamp applies BOTH change-date primitives to each delivered ∩ git-dirty file — the existing top-level bumpFrontmatterLastUpdated AND the new bumpNestedLifecycleLastUpdated (helpers/upgrade-versions.ts v1.2.0) — so a version-gated delivery no longer leaves `lifecycle.last_updated` claiming a pre-delivery date (the co-abap staleness class, ~29 audit warnings fleet-wide per wave); `last_reviewed` is NEVER engine-stamped (DEC-20261029-01 condition 1); the T-20261028-002 deliveredRels ∩ git-dirty restriction stays as the honesty guard. (A3) OPT-IN CHANGELOG SCAFFOLDING: new `--changelog-entry` flag (apply-mode only, default OFF — CHANGELOG.md is project-owned) inserts one editable chore(upgrade) bullet at the top of the project CHANGELOG's first [Unreleased] section (section created when absent) via scaffoldChangelogEntry; idempotent per template version; runs BEFORE the manifest write so the CHANGELOG change rides the recorded delivery (files[] attribution; PIPELINE_ARTIFACT_PATHS membership keeps dev-sync step 3.9 tolerant). (B1) TOMBSTONE_CLEARED (INFO, lib v1.31.0): an applied --clear-tombstone grant that deletes ≥ 1 manifest entry records an auditable decision row naming the glob and the dropped paths; the shipped baseline-drop semantics (v1.79.0, 94a390945) are unchanged and pinned by regression tests (clear → next run absent-copy delivers as NEW, glob-exact drop scope, tombstoneGuard no-fire). (B2) TOMBSTONE PRUNE SYMMETRY: the tombstones carry-forward prunes entries whose template counterpart left the template tree — the exact mirror of the baselines rule (v1.79.0); a template-departed tombstone is provably inert. (D1) STUB_VERSION_DIVERGENCE (INFO, lib v1.31.0): the agents/ STUB skip arm reports a stub whose existing `version` differs from its template counterpart's (backfill never overwrites a sanctioned pin); placed after the primary skip rows so a consumer reading a path's first decision row still sees the verdict. lib/upgrade-policy.ts v1.31.0; helpers/upgrade-versions.ts v1.2.0; tests/unit/upgrade-restamp.test.ts v1.1.0 + tests/unit/upgrade-decision-log.test.ts v1.4.0. Prior: v1.83.0 (2026-10-28, design docs/designs/2026-10-28-instruction-ownership-backport-hardening-design.md D6): `--port-candidates` — the mechanical would-revert inventory. Dry-run-only: apply mode REFUSES the flag (Q3; §5.2's "apply ignores" strengthened to a usage error); in --dry-run --port-candidates mode the pass output is suppressed and the run ends with the report (--json: one {schemaVersion, project, generatedAt, templateVersion, sections, summary} document; default markdown operator view) composed from the data the dry-run already classified — sections conflict_bound (CONFLICT rows: agents/ escalation + D2 census-gated replaces), tombstone_candidates (TOMBSTONED), stub_composed_mismatches (D9 stub-composed drift set), adapted_span_candidates (EVOLVED_REGION census rows), each item {path, surface, trigger, disposition} (§5.2). Report-only exit 0 — it informs the nightly Step 2, nothing gates. Prior: v1.82.0 (2026-10-28, design docs/designs/2026-10-28-instruction-ownership-backport-hardening-design.md D1-D4): instruction-file region census + skeleton widening — mergeWorkspaceManaged (now pass-labeled MERGE/DOCS_MERGE) computes the out-of-zone census for the five instruction files BEFORE the merge and records REGION_IN_SYNC / EVOLVED_REGION / TEMPLATE_AHEAD_REGION rows (zero rows in-sync; Q2: I2 VARIANT-* sections stay project-state; Q1: template-absent blocks stay WARN unless a write channel targets the span); the unlabeled reconcile + skeleton replace escalate census-adapted spans to CONFLICT (ERROR) without --disposition adopt-template (ADOPT row under the grant; D4: decisions name the adjudicated-port procedure); the ADR-0101 skeleton step widens from CLAUDE/GEMINI to all five instruction files (lib v1.2.0, zero entries = no-op). lib/upgrade-policy v1.30.0, lib/managed-block-merge v1.5.0. Prior: v1.81.0 (2026-10-28, T-20261028-013 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-28-instruction-ownership-backport-hardening-design.md D9): stub-composed compare and delivery at upgrade time — when the agents/ pass resolves an extends-stub to its target, the compare AND delivery content is composeResolvedAgentContent(stub, target, variant) (the exact bytes the scaffold §2.3b and the adopt settling pass wrote) instead of the bare extends-target bytes that re-read variant-scoped stub frontmatter (co-design i18n phases:[4], co-develop [5]) as permanent PROJECT_EVOLVED drift; the version gate reads the composed frontmatter (stub version wins — the stub version ladder gates re-delivery), a stub-composed file reaching the equal-version drift arm with templateChanged emits one STUB_VERSION_BUMP_ADVICE (INFO) row (Q5 advisory-only this wave), MERGE_MANAGED files stay excluded, and the v1.80.0 STUB_SKIP/STUB_FM_BACKFILL dead-end branch is unchanged. lib/upgrade-policy.ts v1.29.0 (advisory contract row). Prior: v1.80.0 (2026-10-28, T-20261028-010 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-28-stub-frontmatter-backfill-design.md): STUB FM BACKFILL in the agents/ pass — when a template extends-stub dead-ends in the STUB skip branch (MERGE_MANAGED files such as agents/pm.md, or stubs whose `extends:` target does not resolve) and the PROJECT file at that rel is itself stub-shaped, missing top-level scalar tier/version/last_updated frontmatter keys are backfilled from the template counterpart (last_updated = the delivery date) via lib/upgrade-policy backfillStubFrontmatter (v1.28.0, pure), the stub is written back, the path counts as delivered (TIER NORMALIZED precedent), and one STUB_FM_BACKFILL (INFO) decision row records fields + source. The validate-model-registry v2.1.0 missing-tier FAIL flip (T-20261027-003, template-v0.22.0) turned stale project stubs red — co-price/co-export/co-security agents/pm.md lacked `tier:` (frontmatter predates the field; delivery never touches MERGE_MANAGED pm.md and normalizeAgentTierFrontmatter never ADDS a missing field), so the three stubs were hand-mirrored during the v0.22.0 wave — the delivery pass now does it mechanically. Scope: frontmatter scalars only, bodies untouched (frontmatter ownership for stubs is template-side, body stays project-owned, cf. U-20261028-001); the v1.75.0 extends-target arm is deliberately NOT wired — every untouched branch there either replaces frontmatter wholesale (fields present afterward) or preserves the file under an explicit CONFLICT (ERROR) disposition. Files whose frontmatter already carries the fields are byte-identical (no write, no row). Prior: v1.79.0 (2026-10-28, T-20261028-014 — U-20261028-001 D4 integration defect): BASELINES CARRY-FORWARD at manifest write — the writer rebuilt baselines from the delivered set alone (since T-20261027-004 D1), dropping provenance for every path not delivered this run (conflict-skipped / tombstoned / disposition-skipped); the next run classified them NO_BASELINE and the agents/ equal-version CONFLICT (ERROR) preservation degraded into the overwrite-policy §8 migration-window WARN-deliver (proven end-to-end by the 0.22.1 co-design canary: the conflict-skipped agents/i18n-specialist.md lost its baseline and the follow-up dry-run showed DRIFT-restore WARN — the next wave would clobber the project-canonical phases:[4] at WARN). Rule mirrors the D1 tombstones carry-forward exactly: baselines = (prior ∪ freshly delivered) − paths delivered this run; delivered paths already receive fresh baselines; prior entries whose path left the template tree are pruned (never a classification candidate again). schemaVersion stays 2; the reader side (priorBaselines, classifyProjectState) is unchanged. Prior: v1.78.0 (2026-10-28, U-20261028-001 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-28-delivery-tombstone-ownership-design.md; PR-1a + PR-1b as ONE engine change set, DEC-20261028-02): delivery tombstones + agents/ project-ownership escalation. (D1) TOMBSTONE MANIFEST KEY: the delivery manifest v2 gains the additive optional tombstones key (schemaVersion stays 2) — { [rel]: { hash, templateVersion, recordedAt, reason } } — with the carry-forward rule `tombstones = (prior ∪ newly recorded) − paths delivered this run` (delivering a path is the natural clear event; directory rels expand recursively so a whole-skill delivery clears its sub-file tombstones). (D2/D3) TOMBSTONE GUARD: tombstoneSkip() consults lib/upgrade-policy.ts tombstoneGuard in EVERY absent-copy delivery branch BEFORE the copy — TEMPLATE TREE SYNC SYNC-absent / WORKSPACE seed / ADD_IF_MISSING seed / JSON_MERGE seed, DOCS_OVERWRITE, COMMANDS_SYNC, agents/ NEW, skills/ whole-directory NEW (common + variant passes), VARIANT ASSET DIRS, ENV_SAMPLE. First observation (baseline without a tombstone) logs TOMBSTONED (ERROR) — delivery refused, run exits non-zero (design §4.4 acceptance shape); recorded tombstones log TOMBSTONED_SKIP (INFO). Resolution flags: --tombstone <glob> (repeatable, apply-mode only — records the deletion as intended with --tombstone-reason; the confirmed run honors it immediately) and --clear-tombstone <glob> (drops entries so the next run delivers the path as NEW). Per D3b --disposition adopt-template does NOT override a tombstone — the guard never reads the disposition flag. (D4) AGENTS OWNERSHIP: the agents/ version-gt delivery arm's EVOLVED (WARN) + deliver row is reclassified to CONFLICT (ERROR) regardless of templateChanged (--disposition adopt-template still grants the delivery as ADOPT per the §5 disposition table), and the NO_BASELINE equal-version restore's decision detail names the migration window ("baseline absent — verify this restore against project intent"). (D6) NO resolver/classifier change — resolveClaim, the baseline classifier shapes, and the severity contract's existing rows are untouched. Dry-run reports candidates and persists nothing (the manifest stays apply-mode-only); resync-audit.ts v1.6.0 consumes the census. Prior: v1.77.0 (2026-10-28, T-20261028-002 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-28-ticket-runner-mechanical-batch-design.md §3): POST-DELIVERY RE-STAMP scope — the last_updated re-stamp walks deliveredRels (expanded to files, same recursion as the manifest baseline expansion) intersected with git status instead of every dirty worktree file, so pre-existing user changes open during an upgrade keep their dates; directory-delivered skill roots keep full coverage via the expansion. Prior: v1.76.0 (2026-10-27, T-20261027-004 — spec docs/designs/2026-10-27-sync-overwrite-policy-design.md §4–§7): sync/overwrite policy. (D1) PROVENANCE BASELINES: the delivery manifest (schemaVersion 2, `files` array verbatim) gains per-file baselines {hash, templateVersion} keyed by baselineKeyFor — the next run classifies every version-gated whole-content candidate as UNTOUCHED / IN_SYNC / PROJECT_EVOLVED / NO_BASELINE instead of keying on git-dirty state, so committed project divergence (the U-20261026-005 genus) is mechanically detectable. (D2) DISPOSITION MATRIX: PROJECT_EVOLVED + template-changed under TEMPLATE TREE SYNC / DOCS_OVERWRITE (phase-definitions + security) / VARIANT ASSET DIRS / ENV_SAMPLE / scripts/ + variant-scripts + overlay version-gt / agents/ + skills/ version-gt escalates to CONFLICT severity ERROR — default SKIP (project copy preserved, delivery skipped, decision recorded, non-zero exit with an actionable summary naming the §6 adjudicated-port procedure); `--disposition adopt-template[=glob]` delivers + re-baselines; template-unchanged evolution delivers as EVOLVED (WARN); NO_BASELINE degrades to today's behavior (design §8 rollout — no fleet red). (D3) UNIFIED DECISION LOG: every pass records {path, policy, pass, verdict, severity, baseline?, detail} — SEED_SKIP / STUB_SKIP / SKELETON_COLLISION (D6b, parsed from the skeleton lib's guarded-skip lines) / CONTEXT_PRESERVE / DRIFT_PRESERVE / DRIFT_RESTORED / MERGE / SKIP join the delivery verdicts; manifest v2 persists decisions[] and the composed PR body gains a Decisions section (counts by severity, ERROR items verbatim). agents/ equal-version drift on a PROJECT_EVOLVED file reclassifies to CONFLICT (ERROR) per the design's dedicated row; skills/ DRIFT (preserved) logs DRIFT_PRESERVE (WARN); scripts/ equal-version + CORE-SCRIPT FORK keep the ADR-0085 D3 canonical restore, logged DRIFT_RESTORED (WARN). T-20261027-005 integration: TIER NORMALIZED / TIER DRIFT log ADOPT (INFO), the MERGE-pass TIER CONFLICT keep logs EVOLVED (WARN). Dry-run classifies + logs without writing and previews the non-zero exit. Prior: v1.75.0 (2026-10-27, T-20261027-005): ADR-0102 legacy agent tier normalization in two delivery passes — (a) the MERGE pass normalizes the project agents/pm.md legacy per-platform tier frontmatter block to the template's scalar tier after the managed-block merge (tier block ONLY; a project scalar differing from the template scalar is deliberate divergence → TIER CONFLICT line, untouched); (b) the agents/ pass processes a variant extends-stub's `extends:` target (the resolved template-side counterpart the scaffold wrote into the project) instead of dead-ending, so common-side agent updates reach stub-shielded agents (the 13 variant i18n-specialist.md files; MERGE_MANAGED files stay excluded — their body delivery is the MERGE pass's contract, the v1.49.0 clobber class), and the equal-version drift reconcile recognizes tier-block-only drift with a focused TIER DRIFT verdict before delivering the template copy. Normalizer: lib/upgrade-policy.ts normalizeAgentTierFrontmatter (v1.24.0, pure + idempotent). Prior: v1.85.0 (2026-11-17, design docs/designs/2026-11-17-template-lifecycle-batch-design.md §4.2, DEC-20261117-05; T-20261116-004 batch PR-5): `variant-common-shadow` gains the `zone-override` allowlist class (O-9 Option A, checker-side — no delivery change): for an entry with class zone-override + zones, both sides of the byte comparison have their managed-zone content (VARIANT-ROLE-BOUNDARY in docs/governance/agents/workflows.md; markers follow the `<!-- <zone>-START/END -->` convention of helpers/scaffold-markers.ts and the B-A10 docs-zone generator in propagate-to-templates.ts) replaced with an empty span BEFORE comparing — an in-zone-only delta normalizes byte-equal and goes silent, while divergence reaching outside the zones reports `shadow-out-of-zone-drift` (WARN). Structural assertions join the same check: `shadow-common-zone-dirty` (common's zone missing or carrying content — zone content belongs to variants; CANONICAL_EMPTY_ZONE_CONTENT pins the B-A10 placeholder) and `shadow-zone-nested-markers` (nested managed markers inside variant zone content — a fired nested-marker row suppresses the coarser out-of-zone row for the same instance). A zone-override path missing its markers on either side fail-closes as out-of-zone drift. The tracked class and its commonSha256 guard are unchanged. The co-learning workflows.md allowlist entry converts tracked → zone-override in the same PR (atomic switch, design §4.3); the template copy itself stays untouched. computeShadowSet takes the parsed allowlist entries as a new optional 4th arg; classifyShadows takes the variant dirs as a new optional 4th arg; pure helpers zoneMarkers/stripManagedZones/extractZoneContent/CANONICAL_EMPTY_ZONE_CONTENT exported. Unit-pinned by tests/unit/validate-templates-variant-shadow.test.ts v1.1.0 (seeded-violation + live-tree pins). Version takes 1.85.0 because v1.84.0 was consumed by DEC-20261117-07 (this design's §4.2 originally reserved it). L1 mirror and registry row in lockstep. Exemptions are reported, not silent: per-candidate line in both modes plus a new top-level `exemptions` key in --json.
